        <div class="row">
            <div class="col-md-12">
                <div class="section-title">
                    <h2 class="wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.1s">Lịch sử Đặt lịch</h2>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="col-md-12">
                @if (TempData["ok"] != null)
                {
                    <div class="alert alert-success">@TempData["ok"]</div>
                }
                @if (TempData["err"] != null)
                {
                    <div class="alert alert-danger">@TempData["err"]</div>
                }

                @if (!Model.Any())
                {
                    <div class="alert alert-info">Bạn chưa có lịch hẹn nào.</div>
                    <a href="@Url.Action("Index","Doctors")" class="btn btn-primary">Tìm bác sĩ ngay</a>
                }
                else
                {
                    <div class="table-responsive wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.2s">
                        <table class="table table-striped table-hover">
                            <thead>
                                <tr>
                                    <th>Ngày hẹn</th>
                                    <th>Giờ hẹn</th>
                                    <th>Bác sĩ</th>
                                    <th>Chuyên khoa</th>
                                    <th>Trạng thái</th>
                                    <th>Hành động</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                                @foreach (var item in Model)
                                {
                                    var localTime = item.StartTime.ToLocalTime();

                                    bool canCancel = item.Status != AppointmentStatus.Canceled
                                                     && item.Status != AppointmentStatus.Completed
                                                     && item.StartTime > DateTime.UtcNow;

                                    <tr>
                                        <td>@localTime.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy")</td>
                                        <td>@localTime.ToString("HH:mm")</td>
                                        <td>@(item.Doctor?.Name ?? "N/A")</td>
                                        <td>@(item.Doctor?.Specialty ?? "N/A")</td>
                                        <td>
                                            @* === SỬA LỖI: Đã thêm { } cho các khối if/else === *@
                                            @{
                                                string statusClass = "label-default";
                                                if (item.Status == AppointmentStatus.Pending)
                                                {
                                                    statusClass = "label-warning";
                                                }
                                                else if (item.Status == AppointmentStatus.Confirmed)
                                                {
                                                    statusClass = "label-success";
                                                }
                                                else if (item.Status == AppointmentStatus.Canceled)
                                                {
                                                    statusClass = "label-danger";
                                                }
                                                else if (item.Status == AppointmentStatus.Completed)
                                                {
                                                    statusClass = "label-info";
                                                }
                                            }
                                            <span class="label @statusClass">@item.Status</span>
                                        </td>
                                        <td>
                                            @if (canCancel)
                                            {
                                                using (Html.BeginForm("Cancel", "Bookings", new { id = item.Id }, FormMethod.Post, new { @style="display:inline;" }))
                                                {
                                                    @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                                    <button type="submit" class="btn btn-xs btn-danger"
                                                            onclick="return confirm('Bạn có chắc chắn muốn hủy lịch hẹn lúc @localTime.ToString("HH:mm dd/MM/yyyy")?');">
                                                        Hủy lịch
                                                    </button>
                                                }
                                            }
                                            else
                                            {
                                                <span class="text-muted">—</span>
                                            }
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                }
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                }
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

@model Clinic.Models.Doctor
@{
    ViewBag.Title = "Thông tin bác sĩ";
    Layout = "~/Views/Shared/_Layout.cshtml";

    // === THÊM BIẾN NÀY ĐỂ LẤY MÃ LỊCH HẸN VỪA ĐẶT ===
    int? justBookedId = TempData["apptId"] as int?;

    // (Kiểm tra Model null)
    if (Model == null)
    {
        <section style="padding:60px 0;">
            <div class="container">
                <div class="alert alert-danger">Không tìm thấy thông tin bác sĩ.</div>
                <a href="@Url.Action("Index","Doctors")" class="btn btn-default">← Quay lại danh sách</a>
            </div>
        </section>
        return;
    }

    // Ảnh theo giới tính
    var g = (Model.Gender ?? "").Trim().ToLower();
    var photoSrc = Url.Content(g.StartsWith("nư") || g.StartsWith("nu")
        ? "~/Resources/images/team-image3.jpg"
        : "~/Resources/images/team-image2.jpg");
    var isPatient = Request.IsAuthenticated && User.IsInRole("Patient");
    var bookingUrl = Url.Action("Create", "Bookings", new { doctorId = Model.Id });
    var loginToBookUrl = Url.Action("Login", "Account", new { area = "", ReturnUrl = bookingUrl });
}

<section style="padding:60px 0;">
    <div class="container">

                            @if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model.Email))

                                @:—

                    </div>

                @if (justBookedId.HasValue)
                {
                    <div class="alert alert-info" style="margin-top:12px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
                        <span>Mã lịch hẹn: <strong>#@justBookedId.Value</strong></span>

                        @* Nút Hủy Lịch Vừa Đặt (POST đến Bookings/Cancel) *@
                        @using (Html.BeginForm("Cancel", "Bookings", new { id = justBookedId.Value }, FormMethod.Post))
                        {

                                    onclick="return confirm('Bạn có chắc chắn muốn hủy lịch hẹn này? Thao tác này sẽ chuyển bạn đến trang Lịch sử.');">
                                Hủy lịch hẹn
                            </button>
                        }
                    </div>

                @* === KẾT THÚC SỬA ĐỔI === *@

@model Clinic.Models.DoctorsIndexVm

@{
    ViewBag.Title = "Tìm bác sĩ";
    Layout = "~/Views/Shared/_Layout.cshtml"; // Ensure correct layout

    var isPatient = Request.IsAuthenticated && User.IsInRole("Patient");
    var loginUrl = Url.Action("Login", "Account", new { area = "" });

    Func<string, string> GenderPhoto = (gender) =>
    {
        var g = (gender ?? "").Trim().ToLower();
        var path = g == "nữ" ? "~/Resources/images/team-image3.jpg" : "~/Resources/images/team-image2.jpg";
        return Url.Content(path);
    };

    var count = Model?.Result?.TotalItems ?? 0;
    var infoMsg = count > 0 ? $"Có {count} bác sĩ phù hợp." : "Không tìm thấy bác sĩ nào với tiêu chí hiện tại.";
}

<section id="doctors-list" style="padding:60px 0;">
    <div class="container">
        <div class="row">

            <div class="col-md-12">
                <div class="section-title">
                    <h2 class="wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.1s">Tìm kiếm bác sĩ</h2>
                    <p class="text-muted">@infoMsg</p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="col-md-12">
                @using (Html.BeginForm("Index", "Doctors", FormMethod.Get,
                        new { @class = "row g-2 wow fadeInUp", data_wow_delay = "0.2s" }))
                {
                    <div class="col-md-6 col-sm-8">
                        <input type="text" name="Query" value="@(Model?.Filter?.Query ?? "")"
                               class="form-control" placeholder="Nhập tên bác sĩ…" />
                    </div>
                    <div class="col-md-3 col-sm-4">
                        <select name="Specialty" class="form-control">
                            <option value="">-- Tất cả khoa --</option>
                            @foreach (var s in (Model?.Specialties ?? Enumerable.Empty<string>()))
                            {
                                var selected = (Model?.Filter?.Specialty == s) ? "selected" : "";
                                <option value="@s" @selected>@s</option>
                            }
                        </select>
                    </div>
                    <div class="col-md-3 col-sm-12">
                        <button class="section-btn btn btn-default btn-blue" type="submit" style="width:100%;">
                            Tìm bác sĩ
                        </button>
                    </div>
                }
            </div>

            <div class="clearfix" style="margin-top:15px;"></div>

            @if (Model?.Result?.Items != null && Model.Result.Items.Any())
            {
                foreach (var d in Model.Result.Items)
                {
                    var bookingUrl = Url.Action("Create", "Bookings", new { doctorId = d.Id });
                    var loginToBookUrl = loginUrl + "?ReturnUrl=" + Url.Encode(bookingUrl);
                    var detailUrl = Url.Action("Details", "Doctors", new { id = d.Id });

                    <div class="col-md-4 col-sm-6">
                        <div class="team-thumb wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.1s">
                            <a href="@detailUrl" title="Xem chi tiết @d.Name">
                                <img src="@GenderPhoto(d.Gender)" class="img-responsive" alt="@d.Name" />
                            </a>
                            <div class="team-info">
                                <h3>@d.Name</h3>
                                <p class="text-muted">@d.Specialty</p>
                                <div class="team-contact-info">
                                    @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(d.PhoneNumber))
                                    {<p><i class="fa fa-phone"></i> @d.PhoneNumber</p>}
                                    @if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(d.Email))
                                    {<p><i class="fa fa-envelope-o"></i> <a href="mailto:@d.Email">@d.Email</a></p>}
                                </div>
                                <div style="display:flex; gap:8px; flex-wrap:wrap;">
                                    <a class="section-btn btn btn-default" href="@detailUrl">Xem chi tiết</a>
                                    @if (isPatient)
                                    {<a class="section-btn btn btn-primary" href="@bookingUrl">Đặt lịch</a> }
                                    else
                                    { <a class="section-btn btn btn-primary" href="@loginToBookUrl">Đăng nhập để đặt lịch</a>}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                }
            }
            else
            {
                <div class="col-md-12">
                    <div class="alert alert-info wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.1s" style="margin-top:10px;">
                        Không có dữ liệu hiển thị.
                    </div>
                </div>
            }

            <div class="col-md-12">
                @{
                    var r = Model?.Result;
                    var f = Model?.Filter ?? new Clinic.Models.DoctorsFilterVm();
                    var totalPages = r?.TotalPages ?? 0;
                    var page = r?.Page ?? 1;
                    var start = System.Math.Max(1, page - 2);
                    var end = System.Math.Min(totalPages, page + 2);
                    Func<int, object> link = i => new { Query = f.Query, Specialty = f.Specialty, Page = i, PageSize = f.PageSize };
                }
                @if (totalPages > 1)
                {
                    <nav aria-label="pagination" class="text-center" style="margin-top:10px;">
                        <ul class="pagination">
                            <li class="@(page==1?"disabled":"")"><a href="@Url.Action("Index", link(1))">«</a></li>
                            <li class="@(page==1?"disabled":"")"><a href="@Url.Action("Index", link(page-1 < 1 ? 1 : page-1))">‹</a></li>
                            @for (int i = start; i <= end; i++)
                            {
                                <li class="@(i==page?"active":"")"><a href="@Url.Action("Index", link(i))">@i</a></li>
}
                            <li class="@(page==totalPages?"disabled":"")"><a href="@Url.Action("Index", link(page+1 > totalPages ? totalPages : page+1))">›</a></li>
                            <li class="@(page==totalPages?"disabled":"")"><a href="@Url.Action("Index", link(totalPages))">»</a></li>
                        </ul>
                    </nav>
                }
            </div>

        </div>
    </div>
</section>

@model Clinic.Models.ManageLoginsViewModel
@using Microsoft.Owin.Security
@{
    ViewBag.Title = "Manage your external logins";
}

<main aria-labelledby="title">
<h2 id="title">@ViewBag.Title.</h2>

    <p class="text-success">@ViewBag.StatusMessage</p>
    @{
        var loginProviders = Context.GetOwinContext().Authentication.GetExternalAuthenticationTypes();
        if (loginProviders.Count() == 0) {
            <div>
                <p>
                    There are no external authentication services configured. See <a href="https://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=313242">this article</a>
                    for details on setting up this ASP.NET application to support logging in via external services.
                </p>
            </div>
        }
        else
        {
            if (Model.CurrentLogins.Count > 0)
            {
                <h4>Registered Logins</h4>
                <table class="table">
                    <tbody>
                        @foreach (var account in Model.CurrentLogins)
                        {
                            <tr>
                                <td>@account.LoginProvider</td>
                                <td>
                                    @if (ViewBag.ShowRemoveButton)
                                    {
                                        using (Html.BeginForm("RemoveLogin", "Manage"))
                                        {
                                            @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                                            <div>
                                                @Html.Hidden("loginProvider", account.LoginProvider)
                                                @Html.Hidden("providerKey", account.ProviderKey)
                                                <input type="submit" class="btn btn-outline-dark" value="Remove" title="Remove this @account.LoginProvider login from your account" />
                                            </div>
                                        }
                                    }
                                    else
                                    {
                                        @: &nbsp;
                                    }
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                        }
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            }
            if (Model.OtherLogins.Count > 0)
            {
                using (Html.BeginForm("LinkLogin", "Manage"))
                {
                    @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                    <div id="socialLoginList">
                    <p>
                        @foreach (AuthenticationDescription p in Model.OtherLogins)
                        {
                            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-default" id="@p.AuthenticationType" name="provider" value="@p.AuthenticationType" title="Log in using your @p.Caption account">@p.AuthenticationType</button>
                        }
                    </p>
                    </div>
                }
            }
        }
    }
</main>

@model Clinic.Models.Patient
@{
    // Layout của Patient (layout public)
    ViewBag.Title = ViewBag.Title ?? "Hồ sơ Bệnh nhân"; // Lấy Title từ Controller hoặc đặt mặc định
    Layout = ViewBag.Layout ?? "~/Views/Shared/_Layout.cshtml"; // Lấy Layout từ Controller hoặc mặc định

    // Biến để biết đây là form đăng ký mới, bắt buộc hoàn thiện, hay chỉnh sửa
    var isNewRegistration = ViewBag.IsNewRegistration as bool? ?? false;
    var isForcedCompletion = ViewBag.IsForcedCompletion as bool? ?? false;
    var isEditing = !isNewRegistration && !isForcedCompletion; // Đang chỉnh sửa nếu không phải 2 trường hợp kia

    // Tiêu đề form động
    string formTitle = isNewRegistration ? "Hoàn thiện Hồ sơ Bệnh nhân"
                      : isForcedCompletion ? "Hoàn thiện Hồ sơ Bệnh nhân"
                      : "Chỉnh sửa Hồ sơ";
    // Văn bản nút Submit động
    string submitButtonText = isNewRegistration ? "Hoàn tất đăng ký"
                             : isForcedCompletion ? "Hoàn tất hồ sơ"
                             : "Lưu thông tin";
    // Văn bản hướng dẫn động
    string instructionText = isNewRegistration ? "Vui lòng nhập đầy đủ thông tin để hoàn tất đăng ký tài khoản."
                            : isForcedCompletion ? "Vui lòng nhập đầy đủ thông tin để tiếp tục sử dụng dịch vụ."
                            : "Cập nhật thông tin cá nhân của bạn.";
}

@model Clinic.Models.ReviewCreateViewModel
@{
    ViewBag.Title = "Viết đánh giá";
    Layout = "~/Views/Shared/_Layout.cshtml"; // Use the main site layout
}

<section id="review-create" style="padding: 60px 0;">
    <div class="container" style="max-width: 720px;">
        <div class="row">
            <div class="col-md-12">
                <div class="section-title">
                    <h2 class="wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.1s">Viết đánh giá cho Lịch hẹn</h2>
                    @* Display appointment info for context *@
                    <p>
                        Lịch hẹn với Bác sĩ: <strong>@Model.DoctorName</strong><br />
                        Vào lúc: <strong>@Model.AppointmentDate.ToString("HH:mm dd/MM/yyyy")</strong>
                    </p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="col-md-12">
                @using (Html.BeginForm("Create", "Reviews", FormMethod.Post))
                {
                    @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                    @Html.HiddenFor(m => m.AppointmentId)
                    @Html.HiddenFor(m => m.DoctorName) @* Keep hidden values for repopulation if error *@
                    @Html.HiddenFor(m => m.AppointmentDate)

                    @* Display general validation errors (like "Already reviewed") *@
                    @Html.ValidationSummary(true, "", new { @class = "text-danger" })

                    @* Star Rating Input *@
                    <div class="form-group wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.2s">
                        @Html.LabelFor(m => m.Rating, "Mức độ hài lòng (1-5 sao)")
                        <div class="rating-stars">
                            @* Use hidden radio buttons and star labels ★ *@
                            @for (int i = 5; i >= 1; i--)
                            {
                                @* Mark as 'checked' if value matches Model.Rating *@
                                <input type="radio" id="star@(i)" name="Rating" value="@i" @(Model.Rating == i ? "checked" : "") required />
                                <label for="star@(i)" title="@(i) sao">★</label>
                            }
                        </div>
                        @* Display validation error for Rating if any *@
                        @Html.ValidationMessageFor(m => m.Rating, "", new { @class = "text-danger" })
                    </div>

                    @* Comments Input *@
                    <div class="form-group wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.3s">
                        @Html.LabelFor(m => m.Comments)
                        @Html.TextAreaFor(m => m.Comments, new { @class = "form-control", rows = 5, placeholder = "Chia sẻ cảm nhận của bạn..." })
                        @Html.ValidationMessageFor(m => m.Comments, "", new { @class = "text-danger" })
                    </div>

                    @* Submit and Cancel Buttons *@
                    <div class="form-group wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.4s">
                        <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary">Gửi đánh giá</button>
                        <a href="@Url.Action("ListReviewable", "Reviews")" class="btn btn-default">Quay lại</a> @* Link back to the list *@
                    </div>
                }
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</section>

@* Add CSS for star rating - Ideally, put this in your site's main CSS file *@
@section Styles {
    <style>
        .rating-stars {
            display: inline-block;
            direction: rtl;
        }
            /* Reverse direction for hover effect */
            .rating-stars > input {
                display: none;
            }
            /* Hide radio buttons */
            .rating-stars > label {
                color: #ddd; /* Inactive star color */
                font-size: 2em;
                cursor: pointer;
                transition: color 0.2s;
                padding: 0 2px;
            }
            /* Color stars when input is checked or label/previous labels are hovered */
            .rating-stars > input:checked ~ label,
            .rating-stars:not(:checked) > label:hover,
            .rating-stars:not(:checked) > label:hover ~ label {
                color: #f5b301; /* Active/hover star color */
            }
                /* Special hover effect when already checked */
                .rating-stars > input:checked + label:hover,
                .rating-stars > input:checked ~ label:hover,
                .rating-stars > label:hover ~ input:checked ~ label,
                .rating-stars > input:checked ~ label:hover ~ label {
                    color: #f59e0b; /* Slightly darker hover color */
                }
    </style>
}

@model IEnumerable<Clinic.Models.Appointment>
@using Clinic.Models 
@{
    ViewBag.Title = "Chọn Lịch hẹn để Đánh giá";
    Layout = "~/Views/Shared/_Layout.cshtml"; 

    var reviewedIds = ViewBag.ReviewedIds as HashSet<int> ?? new HashSet<int>();
}

<section id="reviewable-list" style="padding: 60px 0;">
    <div class="container">
        <div class="row">
            <div class="col-md-12">
                <div class="section-title">
                    <h2 class="wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.1s">@ViewBag.Title</h2>
                    <p>Chỉ những lịch hẹn đã hoàn thành và kết thúc mới có thể đánh giá.</p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="col-md-12">
                @if (!Model.Any())
                {
                    <div class="alert alert-info wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.2s">
                        Bạn chưa có lịch hẹn nào đã hoàn thành để đánh giá.
                    </div>
                }
                else
                {
                    <div class="table-responsive wow fadeInUp" data-wow-delay="0.2s">
                        <table class="table table-striped table-hover">
                            <thead>
                                <tr>
                                    <th>Ngày hẹn</th>
                                    <th>Giờ hẹn</th>
                                    <th>Bác sĩ</th>
                                    <th>Trạng thái ĐG</th>
                                    <th>Hành động</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                                @foreach (var item in Model)
                                {
                                    var localTime = item.StartTime.ToLocalTime();
                                    bool hasBeenReviewed = reviewedIds.Contains(item.Id);

                                    <tr>
                                        <td>@localTime.ToString("dd/MM/yyyy")</td>
                                        <td>@localTime.ToString("HH:mm")</td>
                                        <td>@(item.Doctor?.Name ?? "N/A")</td>
                                        <td>
                                            @if (hasBeenReviewed)
                                            {
                                                <span class="label label-success">Đã đánh giá</span>
                                            }
                                            else
                                            {
                                                <span class="label label-default">Chưa đánh giá</span>
                                            }
                                        </td>
                                        <td>
                                            @if (!hasBeenReviewed)
                                            {
                                                <a href="@Url.Action("Create", "Reviews", new { appointmentId = item.Id })" class="btn btn-xs btn-info">
                                                    Viết đánh giá
                                                </a>
                                            }
                                            else
                                            {
                                                <span class="text-muted">—</span>
                                                @* Optional: Add a "View Review" link here later *@
                                            }
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                }
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                }
                <div style="margin-top: 20px;">
                    <a href="@Url.Action("Index", "Bookings")" class="btn btn-default">← Quay lại Lịch sử</a>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</section>
.

E:\Study\WebApp\Project\Private-Clinic\Clinic\Views\Shared\_Header.cshtml
--------------------------
@using Microsoft.AspNet.Identity
@{
    var isPatient = Request.IsAuthenticated && User.IsInRole("Patient");
    var controller = (ViewContext.RouteData.Values["controller"] as string ?? "").ToLower();
    var action = (ViewContext.RouteData.Values["action"] as string ?? "").ToLower();
    var isHomePage = controller == "home" && action == "index";
    var homeUrl = Url.Action("Index", "Home");
}

<section class="navbar navbar-default navbar-static-top" role="navigation">
    <div class="container">
        <div class="navbar-header">
            <button class="navbar-toggle" data-toggle="collapse" data-target=".navbar-collapse">
                <span class="icon icon-bar"></span>
                <span class="icon icon-bar"></span>
                <span class="icon icon-bar"></span>
            </button>


            <a href="@Url.Action("Index","Home")" class="navbar-brand">
                <i class="fa fa-h-square"></i>ealth Center
            </a>
        </div>

        <div class="collapse navbar-collapse">
            <ul class="nav navbar-nav nav-left">
                @* HOME *@
                @if (isHomePage)
                {
                    <li><a href="#home" class="smoothScroll">Home</a></li>
                }
                else
                {
                    <li><a href="@homeUrl#home">Home</a></li>
                }

                @* ABOUT US *@
                @if (isHomePage)
                {
                    <li><a href="#about" class="smoothScroll">About Us</a></li>
                }
                else
                {
                    <li><a href="@homeUrl#about">About Us</a></li>
                }

                @* Doctors always shown *@
                <li>@Html.ActionLink("Doctors", "Index", "Doctors")</li>

                @* === MODIFICATION START === *@
                @* Show History/Review for Patient, OR Make Appointment for Guest *@
                @if (isPatient)
                {
                    <li>@Html.ActionLink("History", "Index", "Bookings")</li>
                    <li>@Html.ActionLink("Write Review", "ListReviewable", "Reviews")</li>
                }
                else // Only show Make an Appointment if NOT a patient (i.e., guest)
                {
                    <li>@Html.ActionLink("Make an appointment", "Index", "Appointment")</li>
                }
                @* === MODIFICATION END === *@
            </ul>


            <ul class="nav navbar-nav navbar-right nav-right">
                @Html.Partial("_LoginPartial") @* Renders Login/Register or User dropdown *@
            </ul>
        </div>
    </div>
</section>
.

E:\Study\WebApp\Project\Private-Clinic\Clinic\Views\Shared\_LoginPartial.cshtml
--------------------------
@using Microsoft.AspNet.Identity

@if (!Request.IsAuthenticated)
{
    <li class="appointment-btn">
        <a href="@Url.Action("Login","Account", new { area = "" })"
           class="section-btn btn btn-blue">Đăng nhập</a>
    </li>
    <li class="appointment-btn">
        <a href="@Url.Action("Register","Account", new { area = "" })"
           class="section-btn btn btn-primary">Đăng ký</a>
    </li>
}
else
{
    <li class="appointment-btn dropdown">
        <a href="#" class="section-btn btn btn-primary dropdown-toggle" data-toggle="dropdown">
            Xin chào, @User.Identity.GetUserName() <span class="caret"></span>
        </a>
        <ul class="dropdown-menu dropdown-menu-right">

            @* Ensure these are standard links without background classes *@
           
            <li>@Html.ActionLink("Tài khoản của tôi", "Index", "Manage", routeValues: new { area = "" }, htmlAttributes: null)</li>
            @* Replaced the previous plain <a> tag with ActionLink for consistency *@

            <li class="divider"></li>
            <li>
                @using (Html.BeginForm("LogOff", "Account", FormMethod.Post, new { id = "logoutForm", area = "" }))
                {
                    @Html.AntiForgeryToken()
                    @* This button uses btn-link to look like text *@
                    <button type="submit" class="btn btn-link" style="width:100%;text-align:left; padding: 3px 20px; color: #333; background: none; border: none;">Đăng xuất</button>
                }
            </li>
        </ul>
    </li>
}
